## Assistant <sup>2026/5/7 00:25:38</sup>

I've identified the bugs. Here's the analysis:

**Bug 1 — Log type creation fails:** The `log_types_api.py` create endpoint doesn't generate the required `id` (String PK) and `code` (NOT NULL) fields. The `ingestion.py` route handles this correctly with `generate_id()`, but the `log_types_api.py` route (which the frontend actually calls) doesn't.

**Bug 2 — Pipeline creation fails:** The `pipelines.py` create endpoint validates `format_id` against the `FormatTemplate` DB table. But the SmartParser sends preset format IDs (like `"preset_nginx_access"`) that only exist in-memory, not in the DB — causing validation to reject the request. The endpoint also ignores `log_type_id` and `output_table_id` which the model now supports.

Let me fix both backends.

## Assistant <sup>2026/5/7 00:34:44</sup>

All changes are complete. Here's a summary of the bugs found and fixed:

## Root Causes

### Bug 1: Log type creation fails — missing required DB fields

**File:** `server-python/app/routes/log_types_api.py`

The `LogType` model has `id` (String PK) and `code` (NOT NULL), but the create endpoint didn't generate either. This caused a database error on every create attempt. The parallel route in `ingestion.py` handled this correctly, but the `log_types_api.py` route (which the frontend actually calls via `/api/log-types-api/log-types`) did not.

**Fixes:**
- Auto-generate `id` using `uuid` (format: `lt_xxxxxxxx`)
- Auto-generate `code` from the name + short UUID
- Fix URL converters from `<int:lt_id>` to `<lt_id>` (IDs are strings, not integers)
- Fix search query (was referencing non-existent `LogType.pattern` column)
- Accept `page_size` parameter in addition to `limit`
- Add proper model fields to create/update (icon, color, pipeline_id, etc.)

### Bug 2: Pipeline creation fails — format template validation rejects presets

**File:** `server-python/app/routes/pipelines.py`

The create endpoint validated `format_id` against the `FormatTemplate` DB table. But the SmartParser sends preset format IDs (like `"preset_nginx_access"`, `"json"`, `"syslog"`) that only exist in-memory, causing the request to be rejected with "格式模板不存在".

**Fixes:**
- Only fail `format_id` validation for numeric IDs that don't exist in the DB
- Add `log_type_id` and `output_table_id` to the Pipeline create/update constructors
- Same validation fix applied to the update endpoint

### Bug 3: Silent failure masking in SmartParser

**File:** `web/src/components/SmartParser.tsx`

The `handleAdd` function caught API errors silently and created a **local-only** pipeline with `Date.now()` as the ID. The pipeline appeared in the UI but was never persisted — disappearing on refresh. The user had no idea the save actually failed.

**Fixes:**
- Removed the silent fallback; errors now show an `alert()` to the user
- Added name validation before submitting
- Applied same fix to `handleEdit`

### Type fixes
- `web/src/services/api.ts`: Updated `logTypesApi` methods to accept `number | string` for IDs
- `web/src/components/LogTypeManager.tsx`: Updated `LogType` interface to use `number | string` for ID fields

     ```python
     # Fixed CEF parser:
     tokens = extension.split()
     for token in tokens:
         if '=' in token:
             key, _, val = token.partition('=')
             result[key.strip()] = val.strip()
     
     # Added kv extraction for syslog:
     @staticmethod
     def _extract_kv_from_message(message: str) -> Dict[str, str]:
         kv = {}
         for m in re.finditer(r'(\w+)=("[^"]*"|\'[^\']*\'|\S+)', message):
             key = m.group(1)
             val = m.group(2).strip('"\'')
             if val and len(val) < 500: kv[key] = val
         return kv
     ```

## Assistant <sup>2026/5/7 01:31:55</sup>

Bug fixed. The `create_event_action` function at `events_api.py:442` was the only route in the file that didn't handle both numeric IDs and `event_code` strings — it only tried `filter_by(event_code=event_id)`, so numeric ID `4` would look for `event_code='4'` which never exists.

**Fix applied** (2 changes in `events_api.py`):
1. **Line 442**: Added the `event_id.isdigit()` check pattern used by every other route in the file
2. **Line 457**: Changed `event_id=event_id` to `event_id=event.event_code` so the EventAction record always references the proper `event_code` FK value

Verified working: creating actions with numeric ID `4` now resolves to `EVT-20260506-004` and stores the correct reference.

## Assistant <sup>2026/5/7 09:02:14</sup>

All tasks complete. Here's a summary of what was built:

---

## 新增能力总结

### 1. 剧本执行引擎 (`app/engine/playbook_engine.py`)

真实可执行的剧本引擎，替代了原有的空壳返回：

- **拓扑排序执行**：根据 `nodes` 和 `edges` 构建执行图，按依赖顺序执行
- **条件分支**：支持 12 种条件运算符（equals, contains, greater_than, in_list, regex_match 等），条件失败时走 false 分支
- **上下文传递**：节点间通过 `ActionContext` 共享变量，支持 `{{event.src_ip}}` 模板解析
- **执行追踪**：每个节点的执行结果（成功/失败/消息/数据）都被记录到 `PlaybookExecution` 模型

### 2. 动作处理器 (`app/engine/actions.py`)

9 个内置动作，均可直接在剧本中使用：

| 动作 | 说明 |
|------|------|
| `block_ip` | IP 封堵（iptables / 外部设备 API / 模拟） |
| `unblock_ip` | IP 解封 |
| `quarantine_host` | 主机隔离（封堵IP + 标记） |
| `send_notification` | 发送系统通知 |
| `webhook` | 调用外部 Webhook |
| `run_script` | 执行 Python/Bash 脚本 |
| `update_event` | 更新事件状态/严重度 |
| `add_tag` | 添加标签到事件 |
| `enrich_ip` | IP 威胁情报富化（支持 VirusTotal） |

### 3. 规则评估引擎 (`app/engine/rule_engine.py`)

真实查询 TimescaleDB `parsed_logs` 的规则引擎：

- **threshold 规则**：WAF 外部IP在5分钟内攻击 > 200次 → 生成事件
- **aggregation 规则**：统计聚合（count/sum/avg/distinct_count）
- **correlation 规则**：多条件关联检测（AND/OR 逻辑）
- **query 规则**：自定义 SQL 查询，灵活度最高
- **自动生成事件**：规则命中时自动在 PostgreSQL 创建 Event，出现在工作台

### 4. 预置剧本和规则

**剧本**：
- `IP 封堵处置剧本`：trigger → 判断外部IP → 封堵IP → 通知 → 更新事件 → 打标签
- `事件关闭-IP解封剧本`：trigger → 解封IP → 关闭事件 → 通知

**规则**：
- `WAF 外部IP高频攻击检测` (RULE-2026-001)：5分钟内同IP被WAF拦截 > 200次 → 生成 high 事件
- `大流量数据外传检测` (RULE-2026-002)：10分钟内同主机对外上传异常 → 生成 critical 事件
- `暴力破解检测-SSH登录失败` (RULE-2026-003)：5分钟内同IP对多台主机SSH失败 > 30次 → 生成 high 事件

### 5. API 端点

**剧本**：
- `POST /api/playbooks-api/playbooks/<id>/execute` - 真实执行
- `GET /api/playbooks-api/playbooks/actions/list` - 可用动作列表
- `GET /api/playbooks-api/playbooks/blocked-ips` - 封堵IP列表
- `DELETE /api/playbooks-api/playbooks/blocked-ips/<ip>` - 解封
- `GET /api/playbooks-api/playbooks/executions` - 执行历史
- `POST /api/event-actions-api/events/<id>/playbooks/<pid>/execute` - 事件上执行剧本

**规则**：
- `POST /api/rules-api/rules/<id>/test` - 真实规则测试
- `POST /api/rules-api/rules/evaluate-all` - 评估所有启用规则

### 6. 新增数据库表
- `parsed_logs` (TimescaleDB/PostgreSQL 5433) - 解析日志存储
- `alert_events` (TimescaleDB/PostgreSQL 5433) - 规则触发的告警事件

| 规则ID | 类型 | 严重度 | 规则名称 |
|--------|------|--------|----------|
| RULE-2026-001 | threshold | high | WAF 外部IP高频攻击检测 (≥200次/5min) |
| RULE-2026-004 | threshold | **critical** | WAF SQL注入批量探测检测 (≥100次/5min) |
| RULE-2026-005 | threshold | high | WAF XSS跨站脚本攻击检测 (≥50次/10min) |
| RULE-2026-006 | threshold | high | WAF 目录遍历/文件包含攻击检测 (≥30次/5min) |
| RULE-2026-007 | threshold | **critical** | WAF 远程命令执行(RCE)攻击检测 (≥20次/5min) |
| RULE-2026-008 | threshold | **critical** | WAF 单IP高频CC攻击检测 (≥500次/1min) |
| RULE-2026-009 | correlation | high | WAF 多类型攻击扫描器检测 (同时触发SQL+XSS) |
| RULE-2026-010 | query | high | WAF 境外异常访问来源检测 (≥100次/30min) |

覆盖场景：
- **OWASP Top 10**：SQL注入、XSS、目录遍历、RCE命令执行
- **DDoS/CC**：单IP超高频请求攻击
- **扫描器识别**：关联分析多攻击类型，识别自动化扫描工具
- **地域风险**：境外IP异常大量拦截

所有规则均为 `enabled` 状态，可立即在规则管理页面使用 `POST /api/rules-api/rules/evaluate-all` 进行评估，命中后自动在工作台生成安全事件。

```
1	// API 基础路径配置
2	// 开发环境：优先走 webpack 代理（同源），失败时 fallback 到后端直连
3	const API_BASE = '/api';

5	
6	interface RequestOptions {
7	  method?: string;
8	  body?: any;
9	  headers?: Record<string, string>;
10	}
11	
12	export async function request<T = any>(
13	  endpoint: string,
14	  options: RequestOptions = {}
15	): Promise<T> {
16	  const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
17	  const headers: Record<string, string> = {
18	    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
19	    ...options.headers,
20	  };
21	  
22	  if (token) {
23	    headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${token}`;
24	  }
25	
26	  const fetchOptions = {
27	    method: options.method || 'GET',
28	    headers,
29	    body: options.body ? JSON.stringify(options.body) : undefined,
30	  };
```

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - **Phase 1**: Optimize playbook orchestration and rule management. Make playbooks executable so they can trigger real actions (IP blocking via firewall, notification, webhook, etc.) from event context. Make detection rules query TimescaleDB parsed_logs and auto-generate events in the workbench when threats are detected (e.g., WAF external IP attacks >200 times in 5 minutes).
   - **Phase 2**: Create several WAF-related detection rules in the rule management system.
   - **Phase 3**: Simplify the global system configuration page (GlobalConfig.tsx), particularly fix the storage config section which incorrectly references Elasticsearch when the actual platform only uses TimescaleDB + PostgreSQL.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Playbook Execution Engine: Topological sorting of nodes/edges, ActionContext with `{{event.field}}` template resolution, 12 condition operators, branch-based flow
   - Action Handlers: IPBlockManager (iptables/API/mock), 9 actions each implementing `execute(config, context) -> ActionResult`
   - Rule Engine: threshold (count > N in time window with GROUP BY), aggregation (count/sum/avg/distinct_count), correlation (multi-condition AND/OR), query (raw SQL)
   - SQLAlchemy JSON mutation tracking: Must use `flag_modified(model, 'column')` when modifying JSONB dicts in-place
   - TimescaleDB hypertables: `parsed_logs` and `alert_events` tables created without TimescaleDB extension (regular PostgreSQL since extension not installed)
   - Flask blueprint architecture: playbooks_api_bp → `/api/playbooks-api`, rules_api_bp → `/api/rules-api`, events_api_bp → `/api/event-actions-api`, config_bp → `/api/config`
   - Dual database: PostgreSQL (5432) for business data, TimescaleDB (5433) for time-series data
   - Frontend: React TypeScript with webpack dev server

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`app/engine/actions.py`** (CREATED) - 9 action handlers + IPBlockManager
     - IPBlockManager maintains in-memory block list with file-based audit log (`logs/ip_blocks.log`)
     - Supports iptables blocking and external device API blocking
     - Key classes: BlockIPAction, UnblockIPAction, QuarantineHostAction, SendNotificationAction, WebhookAction, RunScriptAction, UpdateEventAction, AddTagAction, EnrichIPAction
     - Fixed tag persistence using `flag_modified(event, 'extra_data')`:

   - **`app/engine/playbook_engine.py`** (CREATED) - PlaybookEngine class
     - `execute(playbook, trigger_type, event, alert, params, executor, request_info) -> Dict`
     - Builds adjacency list from edges, finds start nodes (trigger or indegree=0), executes in order
     - Condition evaluation: `_evaluate_condition` uses CONDITION_OPERATORS dict with 12 operators
     - Stores execution records in PlaybookExecution model, creates EventAction records
     - Template variable resolution via `ActionContext.resolve()`

   - **`app/engine/rule_engine.py`** (CREATED) - RuleEngine class
     - `evaluate_rule(rule) -> Dict` with support for threshold, aggregation, correlation, query types
     - `_build_condition_clause()` converts conditions to SQL WHERE with parameterized queries (preventing injection via field name whitelist)
     - `_create_event_from_match()` generates Event records with event_code EVT-YYYYMMDD-NNN
     - `parse_rule_content()` supports JSON and basic Sigma YAML format
     - `evaluate_all_enabled()` evaluates all enabled rules

   - **`app/routes/playbooks_api.py`** (MODIFIED) - Replaced stub execute with real engine
     - Added endpoints: `GET /playbooks/actions/list`, `GET /playbooks/blocked-ips`, `DELETE /playbooks/blocked-ips/<ip>`, `GET /playbooks/executions`, `GET /playbooks/executions/<id>`
     - Execute endpoint now uses `playbook_engine.execute()` with context from event/alert/params

   - **`app/routes/events_api.py`** (MODIFIED) - `execute_event_playbook` now uses real engine
     - Also accepts both `published` and `enabled` status for playbook execution

   - **`app/routes/rules_api.py`** (MODIFIED) - Test endpoint uses real engine, added evaluate-all
     - `test_rule` now calls `rule_engine.evaluate_rule(rule)`

     - Full file rewritten with proper API integration

     ```typescript
       // 23 items across 5 sections
       { key: 'tsdb.host', section: 'storage', label: 'TimescaleDB地址', type: 'text', value: 'localhost' },
     ```

4. Errors and Fixes:
   - **parsed_logs table doesn't exist**: TimescaleDB only had `alerts` hypertable. Fixed by adding `create_parsed_logs_hypertable()` and `create_alert_events_hypertable()` to timescaledb.py, then initializing via Python script.
   - **TimescaleDB extension not installed**: The hypertable conversion failed because the extension wasn't loaded (only plpgsql). Tables work fine as regular PostgreSQL tables - rule engine queries work correctly.
   - **Tag not persisting in event extra_data**: SQLAlchemy doesn't detect mutations to in-place modified JSONB dicts. Fixed by using `dict(event.extra_data)` copy, `list(extra.get('tags', []))` copy, and `flag_modified(event, 'extra_data')` before commit.
   - **WAF rule #7 creation failed (JSON escaping)**: The SQL query in rule_content contained single quotes that broke the shell curl command. Fixed by using heredoc (`<<'PAYLOAD'`) to avoid escaping issues.
